Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5554 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A glute bridge with a dumbbell on your hips and the knees kept slightly forward. Keeps tension on the glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with your knees bent and feet flat, loaded as the exercise requires.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5554.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest the arms beside the torso with relaxed hands on the documented support surface; this is a balance/staging choice, not an added pressing action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with your knees bent and feet flat, loaded as the exercise requires. 5554.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your shins roughly vertical with the knees kept slightly forward of the hips. 5554.json#/instructions/1
  3. Squeeze your glutes and press your hips up until your body forms a line. 5554.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a second at the top without arching your back. 5554.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower under control and repeat. 5554.json#/instructions/4

5577 — barbell paused sumo deadlift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5577 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Sumo deadlift with a pause just off the floor to build strength out of the bottom.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with a wide stance, toes out, and grip the bar inside your knees. Brace and pull the slack out of the bar.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5577.json#/instructions/0, 5577.json#/instructions/1
